Better: For each d, affected terms are those with i ∈ {a,b} OR j ∈ {a,b}. The union of up to 4 terms; duplicates when both i and j are changed. We can compute unique set by summing over term (i,j) if i in changed set or j in changed set, but if both count once. We can derive by inclusion-exclusion: Sum over i∈{a,b} new h_i v'(i+d) - old h_i v(i+d) plus sum over j∈{a,b} with i=j-d not in {a,b} new h_i v'_j - old. So delta(d) = [terms where i=a,b] + [terms where j=a,b and i not a,b]. For i terms:
T_i(a,d)= new_h_a * new_v_{a+d} - old_h_a*old_v_{a+d}
T_i(b,d)= new_h_b * new_v_{b+d} - old_h_b*old_v_{b+d}
Since old_h_a=0, new_h_a=1; old_h_b=1, new_h_b=0. If valid index.

T_j(b,d): i=b-d not in {a,b}; change = h_i*(new_v_b - old_v_b) = h_i*(1-0) = h_i.

U[x] for all x affected via shifts of those positions: For each changed position p (h changes), v changes opposite. U[x,k] = v[x+k] - h[x-k]. A change at position p by Δh (a +1, b -1; dv = -Δh? h_a 0->1, v_a 1->0; h_b 1->0, v_b 0->1) affects U[x,k] if x+k=p or x-k=p. That's O(n) per position per? For each p, for all x, lags x-p and p-x: two entries per x. So updating U for a swap O(n): for each x 0..n-1, U[x, n-1+(x?)] etc. Specifically v change at position p by δv: affects U[x, lag = p-x] (since x+lag=p) by δv. h change by δh: affects U[x, lag = x-p] (since x-lag=p) by -δh. For a: δh=+1, δv=-1. For b: δh=-1, δv=+1. So update O(n) entries (2n per point, 4n total). This is much cheaper than recompute. Then candidate eval can use updated U. Great.

Objective smooth:
F(h; alpha) = max_l f_l(h) + (1/alpha) logsumexp(alpha*(f_l - max)). Actually to avoid overflow: let m=max f, s=logsumexp(alpha*(f-m)). F = m + s/alpha. Gradient = sum w_l ∇f_l where w_l = exp(alpha*(f-m))/sum, plus derivative of m? Standard: F = (1/α) log ∑ exp(α f_l). Then F = m + (1/α) log∑ exp(α(f_l-m)). Gradient = ∑ p_l ∇f_l, p_l = exp(α(f_l-m))/∑ exp(α(f_l-m)). Yes no derivative of m because ∂m cancels; formula correct for soft logsumexp. For large alpha, p concentrated on max. This F is smooth and >= max, ≤ max+logL/α. minimization approximates. At end evaluate exact max.

- compute U:
```
def compute_U(h):
 n=len(h); L=2*n-1; v=1-h; U=np.zeros((n,L), float)
 for d in range(-(n-1), n):
   k=n-1+d
   if d>=0:
      U[:n-d,k] += v[d:]
      U[d:,k] -= h[:n-d]
   else:
      e=-d
      U[e:,k] += v[:n-e]
      U[:n-e,k] -= h[e:]
 return U
```

1. Initialize binary h, c, U, zeros, ones. M. If C*Mdx < best, update.
2. Iteration loop until deadline:

   - Find best strict swap with eval function. If found, apply.
   - If no strict swap: try neutral/random moves to escape:
      * Evaluate some random pairs to find neutral (newM == M) or slight increase (M+1) if temperature. Apply a random improving/neutral from sample. Or do a random perturbation: swap k random pairs, if final M < M + threshold accept maybe.
      * Or restart from best_h with perturbations.
3. Periodically recompute c from h to correct drift, M.
4. Track best.

Functions:
```
def timeout_check():
    if time.time() > G_DEADLINE: raise RuntimeError
def soft_obj(x):
    timeout_check()
    f = np.correlate(x, 1.0-x, 'full') * G_DX
    m = f.max()
    z = G_ALPHA*(f-m)
    # if alpha large and z very negative, exp underflow to 0; sum_exp maybe 1 for max. Use maxz to be safe
    zm = z.max()
    expz = np.exp(z-zm)
    s = expz.sum()
    return m + (zm + np.log(s))/G_ALPHA
def soft_jac(x):
    timeout_check()
    f = np.correlate(x, 1.0-x, 'full') * G_DX
    m = f.max(); z=G_ALPHA*(f-m); zm=z.max(); expz=np.exp(z-zm); p=expz/expz.sum()
    grad = soft_grad_from_p(x,p)
    return grad
```
But recomputes f. Could cache by comparing x? Not.

`soft_grad_from_p(x,p)`:

```
for idx, w in enumerate(p):
  if w == 0: continue
  d = idx - (n-1)
  if d >=0:
    t1[:n-d] += w*v[d:]
    t2[d:] += w*h[:n-d]
  else:
    e=-d
    t1[e:] += w*v[:n-e]
    t2[:n-e] += w*h[e:]
return dx*(t1-t2)
```

Binary local search implementation in detail:
```
def binary_state_from_h(hf, n, rng):
    target=n//2
    scores=np.asarray(hf,float)
    if scores.size != n: scores=interpolate_to_n(scores,n)
    scores = scores + 1e-7*rng.normal(n)
    idx=np.argsort(scores)[::-1]
    h=np.zeros(n); h[idx[:target]]=1.0
    return h
```

def apply_swap(h,c,U,a,b,zeros,ones,posz,poso):
   # assumes h[a]==0,h[b]==1
   center=n-1; D=b-a

   c[center+D] +=1
   c[center-D] +=1
   h[a]=1; h[b]=0
   update_U(h,U,a,b) # or in place
   # update lists
   remove a from zeros, insert b; remove b from ones, insert a
```

def binary_sa(h, c, U, zeros, ones, posz,poso, currentM, deadline, initial_temp, final_temp, max_steps):
   bestM=currentM; best_h=h.copy()
   rng=...
   T=log decay
   for step in range(max_steps):
      if time>deadline break
      a = zeros[rng.randint(len(zeros))]; b=ones[...]
      # apply c in place
      c += U[a] - U[b]; c[center]-=2; c[center+b-a]+=1; c[center+a-b]+=1
      newM = c.max()
      if newM < currentM or (newM==currentM and rng.rand()<0.3) or np.exp(-(newM-currentM)/T)<rng.rand(): accept
      else revert
   if newM < bestM: update best and state? We can keep state even if best not? For SA, state may be worse but exploraritory; at end we can return best_h but also current? We want use best for descent.
```

Full descent function could be large. Let's outline binary local search driver for a given n and time:
```
def binary_search_n(h_start, n, deadline, mode, rng, time_limit):
   h = binary_from_float(h_start,n,rng)
   state = init_state_binary(h, n)
   best_h,best_M = h.copy(), M
   # local descent loop
   while time < deadline:
      # full best swap if allowed
      if n <= 600:
         found = full_best_swap(...)
      else:
         found = sampled_best_swap(...)
      if found:
         apply_swap(...)
         M = maxM
         if M < best_M: update
         # continue
      else:
         # stuck: do SA for some chunk or perturb
         if time until deadline > 5:
            escaped, state = sa_escape(state, n, deadline_sa, rng)
            if state.M < best_M: update
            # maybe if esc found lower, continue descent; else try perturb/restart
         else: break
```
But we need pass state as separate variables; Python no struct. Could use list/dict? Top-level functions can return tuple. For performance, in driver manage variables and call subfunctions returning updated.

`sa_escape` can take current state and t_end_escape (e.g., 5s or until returns). It will run maybe max_steps and return best state (could be current or improved). Simpler: In stuck, do a perturbation: take best_h, apply k random swaps (or random threshold noise) to get new h, compute state, then descent. This may be more effective than SA from local min. Combine: periodic restart from best with increasing k.

Let's design restart-based algorithm:
For a given n, maintain global best_binary (h,M). Initialize a list of perturbed starts from current best:
- k=0 (current best)
- k=5,10,20,50 random swaps from best
- Also original parent threshold starts.
For each start, run a time-limited descent+SA (e.g., 20-100s) to get local min. Update best. Loop while time left, generating new perturbations of best with maybe larger k. This is typical.

Descent function:
```
def binary_descent_limit(h, n, t_end, rng, full_flag):
   state init
   while time<t_end:
      if full: find full best; else sample
      if found: apply
      else: break
   return best_h,best_M within this descent
```
It descends only, no escape. If reaches local min, returns. Then caller can perturb.

This avoids SA complexity initially. If many local minima, perturbations can escape. But if best is global local min requiring multi-swap barrier, random perturbations may cross. We can also run SA as a descent variant that allows increases. Let's implement both.

`binary_descend` with optional `annealing`: If no strict swap, instead of break, run SA for `anneal_time` then continue descent. This can escape during same run.

Function `interpolate_to_n(parent_h, n)`:
```
def interpolate_to_n(src, n):
    m=src.size
    if m==n: out=src.astype(float,copy=True)
    else:
       xo=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,m,endpoint=False)
       xn=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n,endpoint=False)
       out=np.interp(xn,xo,src.astype(float))
    out=project_feasible(out,n)
    return out
```

Let's write `project_feasible` robust:
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
    h = np.array(h, dtype=float).copy()
    h = np.clip(h,0,1)
    target=n/2.0
    for _ in range(10000):
       s=h.sum()
       diff=target-s
       if abs(diff) < 1e-14: break
       if diff>0:
          room=1-h
          ss=room.sum()
          if ss<1e-15: break
          h += diff/ss*room
       else:
          ss=s
          if ss<1e-15: break
          h += diff/ss*h
    return np.clip(h,0,1)
```
If diff large, one iteration may not exactly due rounding; loop. 10000 enough. But if ss small, maybe stuck. For target feasible, should.

For waterfill projection used in projected gradient maybe not if SLSQP only. We may skip projected gradient.

- Iterate: h = project(h - step*grad). But smooth nonconvex; can get stuck. SLSQP better.

Could use `scipy.optimize.minimize` with `method='L-BFGS-B'` and penalty, as alternative. L-BFGS-B bounds only, often faster than SLSQP for equality approx. We can add for n=500:
- Define penalty objective: F_alpha(h) + lam*(sum - target)^2, bounds. Jac = G + 2 lam (sum-target)*1. Use L-BFGS-B. lam large (1e4). It may violate equality slightly; project. This can be much faster. But equality constraint with penalty may converge to near. Use after SLSQP polish.

```

`make_binary(h_float, n, rng)`:
```
def make_binary(h_float, n, rng):
    h = np.asarray(h_float, dtype=float)
    if h.size != n:
       h = interpolate_to_n(h,n)
    target = n//2
    # add tiny noise to break ties
    h = h + rng.normal(0.0, 1e-6, n)
    # Use argpartition for speed
    # choose top target
    idx = np.argpartition(h, n-target)
    top = idx[n-target:]
    # if ties? partition arbitrary. To ensure top by value, maybe argsort top? Not needed for 1/0 but tie orientation may matter.
    out = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.int8)
    out[top] = 1
    # If sum not target due weird, adjust
    s=out.sum()
    if s != target:
       # fallback argsort
       idx=np.argsort(h)[::-1]
       out=np.zeros(n,dtype=np.int8); out[idx[:target]]=1
    return out
```
`np.argpartition(h, n-target)` returns n-target smallest before, top after. If target=n/2. For n even. Good. But if h has NaN? no.

`binary_initial_c(h)`:
```
def binary_initial_c(h):
    hi = h.astype(np.int64)
    c = np.correlate(hi, 1 - hi, mode='full')
    return c.astype(np.int32, copy=False)
```
`compute_U_int(h)`: h int8.
```
def compute_U_int(h):
    n=h.size; L=2*n-1
    v = (1 - h).astype(np.int8)
    U = np.zeros((n,L), dtype=np.int8)
    h8 = h
    for d in range(-(n-1), n):
       k = n-1+d
       if d >=0:
          U[:n-d, k] += v[d:]
          U[d:, k] -= h8[:n-d]
       else:
          e=-d
          U[e:, k] += v[:n-e]
          U[:n-e, k] -= h8[e:]
    return U
```
Check dtype: `U[:] += h8[:n-d]` where h8 int8. Good.

G_ARANGE = None; G_ARANGE_N = -1
def get_arange(n):
   global G_ARANGE, G_ARANGE_N
   if G_ARANGE_N != n:
      G_ARANGE = np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)
      G_ARANGE_N = n

   n=U.shape[0]; center=n-1; xs=get_arange(n)
   cols1 = center + xs - a
   cols2 = center + a - xs
   cols3 = center + xs - b

   U[:, cols3] += 1

`find_best_swap_full(c,U,zeros,ones,M)` where zeros/ones are np arrays int. Returns (a,b,newM) or (-1,-1,M).
For performance, convert Uo to int32: `Uo = U[ones].astype(np.int32, copy=False)`. `L=c.size; center=n-1; ones_arr = np.asarray(ones, dtype=np.intp)`. We'll pass arrays.
```
def find_best_swap_full(c, U, zeros_arr, ones_arr, M):
    L = c.size; n=(L+1)//2; center=n-1
    Uo = U[ones_arr].astype(np.int32, copy=False)
    best = M
    besta = -1; bestb = -1
    # if no ones/zeros return
    for a in zeros_arr:
        base = c + U[a].astype(np.int32, copy=False)  # c int32, U[a] int8 -> int32 array
        base[center] -= 2
        vals = (base[None, :] - Uo).max(axis=1)
        D = ones_arr - a
        idxD = center + D
        idxM = center - D
        cross = np.maximum(base[idxD] - Uo[:, idxD] + 1,
                           base[idxM] - Uo[:, idxM] + 1)
        tot = np.maximum(vals, cross)
        # Optionally early if min tot couldn't beat best? We can use if tot.min() < best
        local = np.argmin(tot)
        tv = tot[local]
        if tv < best:
            best = tv; besta=int(a); bestb=int(ones_arr[local])
    return besta, bestb, best
```
Promusing int arrays: base = c + U[a] yields int32? U[a] int8, c int32 -> int32. Good. `(base[None,:] - Uo)` int32 array. `vals` int32. `cross` int32. `np.maximum` int32. `tot` int32. `argmin` returns index. If best remains M no improvement.
Could improve speed by if `tot.min()` maybe; argmin already.

def apply_swap_int(c, U, h, a, b):

Descent driver:
```
def binary_descend(h, n, t_end, rng, use_full, sa_time=0.0):
    h = h.copy()
    c,b,U? compute
    lists init
    M
    best_h=h.copy(); best_M=M
    rounds=0
    stuck_time=0.0
    while time.time() < t_end:
        if use_full and n <= 600:
            zeros_arr=np.asarray(zeros, dtype=np.intp) # converting each round O(n). Could maintain arrays? Lists update; converting each round maybe okay 250.
            ones_arr=...
            a,b,nm = find_best_swap_full(c,U,zeros_arr,ones_arr,M)
        else:
            # sampled; need arrays? for random choose from Python lists directly; find_best_sampled with lists
            a,b,nm = find_best_swap_sampled_lists(c,U,zeros,ones,M,rng,sample_pairs)
        if a >=0:
            M = apply_swap_int(c,U,h,a,b)
            # update lists
            lists = update_lists(zeros,ones,posz,poso,a,b)
            if M < best_M: best_M=M; best_h=h.copy()
            rounds=0
            if rounds % 200 ==0: recompute maybe
        else:
            # no improving 1-swap. If sa_time allowed, do SA escape.
            if t_end - time.time() > 3:
                # run SA for small time
                h,c,U,zeros,ones,posz,poso,M = sa_escape(h,c,U,zeros,ones,posz,poso,M,t_end - 2, seed)
                if M < best_M: update
                # after SA, continue descent
            else:
                break
    return best_h,best_M
```

`find_best_swap_sampled_lists(c,U,zeros,ones,M,rng, sample_pairs)`:
```
def find_best_swap_sampled_lists(c,U,zeros,ones,M,rng, sample_pairs):
   L=c.size; center=(L-1)//2; best=M; besta=-1; bestb=-1
   nzeros=len(zeros); nones=len(ones)
   for _ in range(sample_pairs):
      a=zeros[rng.randint(nzeros)]; b=ones[rng.randint(nones)]
      tmp = c + U[a] - U[b]
      tmp[center] -= 2; tmp[center+b-a]+=1; tmp[center+a-b]+=1
      nm = int(tmp.max())
      if nm < best: best=nm; besta=a; bestb=b; if best == M-? can't know global lower; if best == 0? no.
   return besta,bestb,best
```

def run_binary_for_n(start_h, n, t_end, seed, min_time_per_start):
   best_h,best_M = binary_descend(start_h, n, min(t_end, t_end-...), seed, full=True)
   attempt=0
   while time.time() < t_end - 3:
      attempt +=1
      k = min(1 + attempt*3, n//4)  # increase perturb
      hstart = perturb_binary(best_h, k, rng)
      # maybe if k=0 also random noise threshold? But binary perturb enough.
      h2,M2 = binary_descend(hstart, n, min(t_end - 3, time.time()+chunk), seed+attempt, full=True)
      if M2 < best_M: best_h, best_M =...
      # if no improvement for several, try larger k or sampled n?
   return best_h,best_M
```

Let's detail `optimize_continuous(h0, n, t_end, start_alpha_idx=0, maxiter_base)`:
```
def optimize_continuous(h0, n, t_end, seed):
    if n < 50: return
    G_N=n; G_DX=2.0/n; G_TARGET=0.5*n; G_DEADLINE=t_end
    h=project_feasible(h0,n)
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=exact_c5(h,n)
    alphas = [20.0,100.0,500.0,2000.0,10000.0,50000.0]
    n_iter = 600 if n<=300 else 200 if n<=600 else 100
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
    cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':sum_constraint}]
    for alpha in alphas:
       if time.time() > t_end - 20: break
       G_ALPHA=alpha
       try:
          res=minimize(soft_objective,h,jac=soft_gradient,method='SLSQP',bounds=bounds,constraints=cons,options={'maxiter':n_iter,'ftol':1e-12,'disp':False})
          if res.x is not None: h=project_feasible(res.x,n)
       except Exception:
          pass
       c=exact_c5(h,n)
       if c<best_c: best_c=c; best_h=h.copy()
       # maybe L-BFGS penalty polish for this alpha
       if time.time() < t_end - 10:
          G_LAM=1e5
          try:
             res2=minimize(penalty_objective,h,jac=penalty_gradient,method='L-BFGS-B',bounds=bounds,options={'maxiter':n_iter,'ftol':1e-12,'disp':False})
             ...
          except Exception: pass
    project final
    return best_h,best_c
```

def project_sum_box(y, S):
    # using sorting to find lambda
    y = np.asarray(y)
    # Find lambda such that sum clip(y+lambda)=S.
    # lambda between min(0? Use bisection 60)
    lo = min(0.0, S - y.sum()) - 1.0? Actually if y all large, need negative lambda to clip to 1. Use lo = -1e6? Too broad.
    lo = -np.max(y) - 1.0
    hi = 1.0 - np.min(y)
    for _ in range(60): mid=(lo+hi)/2; s=np.clip(y+mid,0,1).sum(); if s>S: hi=mid else lo=mid
    x=np.clip(y+(lo+hi)/2,0,1)
    # adjust exact
    return project_feasible(x, len(x)) # but project_feasible may undo? okay
```

   f, g = compute_f_grad(h, alpha)
   x=h.copy(); fx=f; gx=g
   step=1.0

Let's implement continuous functions in detail.

`soft_values_weights(x)`:
```
def soft_values_weights(x):
    f = np.correlate(x, 1.0 - x, mode='full') * G_DX
    m = float(f.max())
    z = G_ALPHA * (f - m)
    zm = float(z.max())
    expz = np.exp(z - zm)
    s = float(expz.sum())
    p = expz / s
    Fval = m + (zm + math.log(s)) / G_ALPHA
    return Fval, p, m, f
```
But objective only needs Fval; jac needs p. To avoid computing f twice in objective and jac, SLSQP calls separately. Could use a global cache: In `soft_objective`, compute and store p? Not helpful if jac called later with same x, but SLSQP may call jac with slightly different. Simpler separate.
However, `soft_gradient` recomputes f and p; overhead double. Acceptable? For n=500, gradient is dominant; double objective maybe 2x. Could implement `soft_objective` only returns value using a faster `compute_soft_value` (correlate+logsum). `soft_gradient` recomputes. Fine.

`soft_objective`:
```
def soft_objective(x):
    timeout_check()
    f = np.correlate(x, 1.0-x, mode='full') * G_DX
    m = f.max()
    z = G_ALPHA*(f-m)
    zm = z.max()
    s = np.exp(z-zm).sum()
    return float(m + (zm + math.log(s))/G_ALPHA)
```
If G_ALPHA very large, f-m up to 0, z - zm ≤0; exp underflow okay.

`soft_gradient`:
```
def soft_gradient(x):
    timeout_check()
    f = np.correlate(x, 1.0-x, mode='full') * G_DX
    m = f.max(); z=G_ALPHA*(f-m); zm=z.max(); ez=np.exp(z-zm); p=ez/ez.sum()
    n=x.size; grad=np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    if G_ALPHA == 0? Not; alpha>0.
    return soft_grad_from_p(x,p)
```
`soft_grad_from_p`:
```
def soft_grad_from_p(x,p):
    n=x.size; L=p.size; v=1.0-x; h=x
    t1=np.zeros(n,float); t2=np.zeros(n,float)
    center=n-1
    for idx in range(L):
       w = float(p[idx])
       if w <= 1e-15: continue
       d = idx - center
       if d >= 0:
          if d < n:
             t1[:n-d] += w * v[d:]
             t2[d:] += w * h[:n-d]
       else:
          e = -d
          if e < n:
             t1[e:] += w * v[:n-e]
             t2[:n-e] += w * h[e:]
    return G_DX * (t1 - t2)
```
For d=0, slices full length. Good. For d=±(n-1), length1.

Could optimize by skipping p small. Use local variables `t1_set = t1.__setitem__`? Not necessary.

`penalty_objective` and gradient use G_LAM:
```
def penalty_objective(x):
    timeout_check()
    val = soft_objective(x)
    diff = float(x.sum() - G_TARGET)
    return val + 0.5*G_LAM*diff*diff
def penalty_gradient(x):
    timeout_check()
    g = soft_gradient(x)
    diff = float(x.sum() - G_TARGET)
    g += G_LAM * diff
    return g
```

`timeout_check` raises `RuntimeError`. `G_DEADLINE` float.

`sum_constraint(x)`:
```
def sum_constraint(x): return float(x.sum() - G_TARGET)
```

`project_feasible`: as above. Need if n=0? no.

`optimize_continuous`: Need `bounds` list of tuples length n; for n=500, okay. SLSQP may complain if initial x not within bounds exactly; project clips. Set `G_DEADLINE = t_end - 2`. If n>800 maybe skip SLSQP? We'll call n≤600. In function, if n too large, return exact.
```
def optimize_continuous(h0, n, t_end, seed=None):
    if n < 50 or time.time() > t_end - 10:
        h=project_feasible(np.asarray(h0,float),n); return h, exact_c5(h,n)
    G_N=n; G_DX=2.0/n; G_TARGET=0.5*n; G_DEADLINE=max(t_end-2, time.time())
    h=project_feasible(np.asarray(h0,float),n)
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=exact_c5(h,n)
    alphas = [20.0, 100.0, 500.0, 2000.0, 10000.0, 50000.0]
    maxiter = 800 if n<=300 else 300 if n<=600 else 100
    bounds = [(0.0,1.0) for _ in range(n)] # this uses a generator? Not closure/lambda. OK. But rule no closures not generators. Use list comprehension okay? It creates list. Fine.
    cons = [{'type': 'eq', 'fun': sum_constraint}]
    for alpha in alphas:
       if time.time() > t_end - 15: break
       G_ALPHA=alpha
       try:
           res = minimize(soft_objective, h, jac=soft_gradient, method='SLSQP', bounds=bounds, constraints=cons, options={'maxiter': maxiter, 'ftol': 1e-12, 'disp': False})
           if hasattr(res,'x') and res.x is not None:
               h2 = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float)
               if np.all(np.isfinite(h2)):
                   h = project_feasible(h2,n)
       except Exception:
           pass
       c2=exact_c5(h,n)
       if c2 < best_c:
           best_c=c2; best_h=h.copy()
       # penalty polish if time
       if time.time() < t_end - 10:
           G_LAM = 1e5
           try:
              res2 = minimize(penalty_objective, h, jac=penalty_gradient, method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, options={'maxiter': maxiter, 'ftol':1e-12, 'disp': False})
              ...
           except Exception: pass
    h=project_feasible(h,n); c=exact_c5(h,n); if c<best_c update
    return best_h,best_c
```
But `minimize` with method L-BFGS-B does not enforce sum; G_LAM=1e5. If sum violation 0.1, penalty 500, gradient 10000? Actually G_LAM*diff=1e5*0.1=1e4, could be large relative F grad ~0.004, forcing sum. Good. But L-BFGS-B may take many steps; use maxiter same. Maybe skip if n>500.

Note: `minimize` SLSQP with constraints uses `sum_constraint` which uses current G_TARGET. Good.

Potential problem: Soft objective gradient computed with p based on f values. If h infeasible sum but within bounds, okay.

Now binary functions.

`init_index_lists`:
```
def init_index_lists(h):
    zeros=[]; ones=[]; posz={}; poso={}
    for i in range(h.size):
        if h[i] == 0:
           posz[i]=len(zeros); zeros.append(i)
        else:
           poso[i]=len(ones); ones.append(i)
    return zeros,ones,posz,poso
```

`remove_insert_lists`: as above, modify in place, return same lists/dicts maybe.
```
def remove_insert_lists(zeros, ones, posz, poso, a, b):
    ia = posz[a]
    lastz = zeros[-1]
    zeros[ia] = lastz
    posz[lastz] = ia
    zeros.pop()
    del posz[a]
    ib = poso[b]
    lasto = ones[-1]
    ones[ib] = lasto
    poso[lasto] = ib
    ones.pop()
    del poso[b]
    posz[b] = len(zeros)
    zeros.append(b)
    poso[a] = len(ones)
    ones.append(a)
    return zeros, ones, posz, poso
```
If a not in zeros or b not in ones due bug, assume no.

`update_U`:
```
def update_U(U,a,b):
    n=U.shape[0]
    xs=get_arange(n)
    center=n-1
    U[:, center + xs - a] -= 1
    U[:, center + a - xs] -= 1
    U[:, center + xs - b] += 1
    U[:, center + b - xs] += 1
```
Check dtype int32. Good.

`apply_swap`:
```
def apply_swap(c, U, h, a, b):
    n = h.size
    center = n-1
    c += U[a]
    c -= U[b]
    c[center] -= 2
    c[center + b - a] += 1
    c[center + a - b] += 1
    h[a] = 1
    h[b] = 0
    update_U(U, a, b)
    return int(c.max())
```
Need center for c length? c length 2n-1, center n-1. Good.

But if we recompute `c` after `apply_swap`? no.

`find_best_swap_full`: Need zero/one arrays. Use int32 arrays. If len zeros or ones zero return. We can optimize by if `Uo` shape huge; no.
```
def find_best_swap_full(c, U, zeros_arr, ones_arr, M):
    if len(zeros_arr)==0 or len(ones_arr)==0: return -1,-1,M
    center = (c.size - 1)//2
    Uo = U[ones_arr]
    best = M
    besta = -1; bestb = -1
    for ai in range(len(zeros_arr)):
        a = zeros_arr[ai]
        base = c + U[a]
        base[center] -= 2
        vals = (base[None, :] - Uo).max(axis=1)
        D = ones_arr - a
        idxD = center + D
        idxM = center - D
        cross = np.maximum(base[idxD] - Uo[:, idxD] + 1,
                           base[idxM] - Uo[:, idxM] + 1)
        tot = np.maximum(vals, cross)
        local = int(np.argmin(tot))
        tv = int(tot[local])
        if tv < best:
            best = tv; besta = int(a); bestb = int(ones_arr[local])
    return besta,bestb,best
```
Note `base = c + U[a]`: c int, U[a] int, base new int. `base[center]-=2` modifies base; okay. But `base` not c. Good. `vals` max of base - Uo. Uo int. If L large, `base[None,:] - Uo` alloc. Good.
Could there be a swap where a=b? no zero/one distinct. If zeros/ones arrays from current state, yes.

Sampled:
```
def find_best_swap_sampled_lists(c,U,zeros,ones,M,rng,samples):
    if len(zeros)==0 or len(ones)==0: return -1,-1,M
    center=(c.size-1)//2
    best=M; besta=-1; bestb=-1
    nz=len(zeros); no=len(ones)
    for s in range(samples):
       a=zeros[rng.randint(nz)]; b=ones[rng.randint(no)]
       tmp = c + U[a] - U[b]
       tmp[center] -= 2
       tmp[center + b - a] += 1
       tmp[center + a - b] += 1
       nm = int(tmp.max())
       if nm < best:
          best=nm; besta=a; bestb=b
    return besta,bestb,best
```
This alloc tmp each. If n=750 L=1499, samples maybe 4000 => 6M ints 24MB per round, okay. Could reduce if time.

`recompute_state`:

def recompute_state(h):
    n=h.size
    c = binary_initial_c(h)
    U = compute_U(h)
    zeros,ones,posz,poso = init_index_lists(h)
    M = int(c.max()) if c.size else 0
    return c,U,zeros,ones,posz,poso,M
```

`binary_descend`: We'll pass `h` int8/32. It should copy.
Parameters: `n, t_end, seed, full_allowed, sa_budget=5.0, sample_questions?`
```
def binary_descend(h_init, n, t_end, seed, full_allowed, sa_budget=3.0):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    h = np.asarray(h_init, dtype=np.int8).copy()
    c,U,zeros,ones,posz,poso,M = recompute_state(h)
    best_h = h.copy(); best_M = M
    swap_counter = 0
    no_improve_streak = 0
    while time.time() < t_end:
        use_full = full_allowed and n <= 500
        if use_full:
            za = np.asarray(zeros, dtype=np.intp)
            oa = np.asarray(ones, dtype=np.intp)
            a,b,nm = find_best_swap_full(c,U,za,oa,M)
        else:
            samples = 4000 if n <= 900 else 2000
            a,b,nm = find_best_swap_sampled_lists(c,U,zeros,ones,M,rng,samples)
        if a >= 0:
            M = apply_swap(c,U,h,a,b)
            zeros,ones,posz,poso = remove_insert_lists(zeros,ones,posz,poso,a,b)
            swap_counter += 1
            if M < best_M:
                best_M = M; best_h = h.copy()
                no_improve_streak = 0
            else:
                no_improve_streak += 1
            if swap_counter % 2000 == 0:
                c,U,zeros,ones,posz,poso,M = recompute_state(h)
                if M < best_M: update
        else:
            # no improving swap
            if time.time() < t_end - 2 and sa_budget > 0:
                # SA escape for sa_budget seconds
                t_sa = min(t_end - 1, time.time() + sa_budget)
                res = binary_sa(h,c,U,zeros,ones,posz,poso,M,t_sa,seed+swap_counter)
                h,c,U,zeros,ones,posz,poso,M = res
                if M < best_M:
                    best_M=M; best_h=h.copy()
            else:
                break
    return best_h, best_M
```
But after SA, `res` returns best state recomputed. If no improvement, returns same best local; then loop continues? It will again find no improving swap and maybe SA again, repeating. Need avoid infinite. We can make binary_descend return when no strict swap and SA does not improve maybe. But SA might improve current M but not best? If returns best state not improved, stuck. We should break if no improvement during SA and no remaining starts. So binary_descend should track if SA result M < M_before_sa or best. If not, break. Modify:
```
        else:
           if time...:
              M_before = M
              res = binary_sa(... limit)
              assign
              if M >= M_before: # no improvement from current
                  # current local min again; break to let caller restart
                  break
              else: continue
           else: break
```
But SA may improve M temporarily but final best not lower than M_before? It returns best_h (lowest state during SA). If lowest == M_before, no escape. Break. Good.

`binary_sa` implementation returns state (recomputed from best_h). It may be expensive to copy best_h every improvement; fine.
```
def binary_sa(h,c,U,zeros,ones,posz,poso,M,t_end,seed):
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    n=h.size; start_t=time.time(); duration=max(1.0,t_end-start_t)
    T0=1.0; Tmin=0.05
    bestM=M; best_h=h.copy()
    while time.time() < t_end:
       a=zeros[rng.randint(len(zeros))]; b=ones[...]
       D=b-a; center=n-1
       c += U[a]; c -= U[b]; c[center]-=2; c[center+D]+=1; c[center-D]+=1
       newM=int(c.max())
       dm=newM-M
       accept=False
       if dm < 0: accept=True
       elif dm == 0: accept = rng.rand() < 0.35
       else:
           elapsed = time.time()-start_t
           frac = min(1.0, elapsed/duration)
           T = T0 * (Tmin/T0) ** frac
           if dm <= 3:
              prob = math.exp(-float(dm)/max(T,1e-6))
              accept = rng.rand() < prob
       if accept:
           M=newM; h[a]=1; h[b]=0; update_U(U,a,b); remove_insert_lists(...)
           if M < bestM:
              bestM=M; best_h=h.copy()
       else:
           c -= U[a]; c += U[b]; c[center]+=2; c[center+D]-=1; c[center-D]-=1
    # return state of best_h
    c,U,zeros,ones,posz,poso,M = recompute_state(best_h)
    return best_h,c,U,zeros,ones,posz,poso,M
```
If SA accepts equal moves, it may wander. It returns best found. If bestM = initial M, break? binary_descend sees M_before maybe initial, after SA recompute M=initial (if no improvement) -> break. Good.

Potential problem: In binary_sa, if it accepts many equal moves, state may change but best remains; best_h initial. It returns initial, losing any potential basin. But if it didn't find lower, okay. To allow other basins, caller can perturb best randomly instead. Good.

`perturb_binary`:
```
def perturb_binary(h, k, rng):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.int8).copy()
    zeros,ones,posz,poso = init_index_lists(h)
    for _ in range(k):
       if len(zeros)==0 or len(ones)==0: break
       a=zeros[rng.randint(len(zeros))]; b=ones[rng.randint(len(ones))]
       h[a]=1; h[b]=0
       zeros,ones,posz,poso = remove_insert_lists(zeros,ones,posz,poso,a,b)
    return h
```
This does arbitrary swaps preserving count. Could create bad states; local descent improves.

`run_binary_n`:
```
def run_binary_n(start_h, n, t_end, seed, full_allowed=True):
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    best_h,best_M = binary_descend(start_h,n,min(t_end, time.time()+?), seed, full_allowed, sa_budget=5.0)
    attempt=0
    while time.time() < t_end - 5:
       attempt +=1
       # choose k perturb: start 5, then increase
       k = min(5 + 3*attempt, n//3)
       # occasionally also use parent threshold noise? If start_h not stored? We have best_h; perturb it.
       hstart = perturb_binary(best_h, k, rng)
       # time chunk: for n<=500 15s, n=750 8s, n>=1000 5s
       if n <= 300: chunk=8.0
       elif n <= 500: chunk=20.0
       elif n <= 800: chunk=12.0
       else: chunk=8.0
       sub_end = min(t_end - 2, time.time() + chunk)
       h2,M2 = binary_descend(hstart,n,sub_end, seed+1000*attempt, full_allowed=(n<=500), sa_budget=3.0)
       if M2 < best_M:
          best_M=M2; best_h=h2
          # reset attempt? keep increasing
       else:
          # if many no improve, increase k more
          if attempt % 5 == 0:
             k = min(k*2, n//2)
             hstart = perturb_binary(best_h, k, rng)
             h2,M2 = binary_descend(hstart,n,sub_end,seed+2000*attempt,full_allowed=(n<=500),sa_budget=3.0)
             if M2<best_M: update
    return best_h,best_M,(2.0/n)*best_M
```
But if best_M from initial descent already local, perturb by 5 swaps may not be enough; we increase. Good.

However, binary_descend may spend its entire chunk even if stuck because it does SA once then break. If no improving swap initially, it will SA for sa_budget then break, returning. So chunk not fully used. Good.

Maybe we should also try starting from parent threshold with random noise each n, not just perturb best. In `run`, for n=500, prepare a few starts and run `binary_descend` on each with time chunks, updating a local best. Instead of `run_binary_n` only perturbing best, we can have a pool.

def random_soft_start(n, rng):
    x = np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n,endpoint=False)
    h = 0.5 + 0.35*np.sin(2*np.pi*rng.randint(2,8)*x + rng.uniform(0,6.28)) + 0.25*np.sin(2*np.pi*rng.randint(3,12)*x + rng.uniform(0,6.28))
    h += 0.1*rng.normal(size=n)
    return project_feasible(h,n)
```

def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):
    t0=time.time(); usable=budget_s-5; dl=t0+budget_s-3
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    parent = get parent if available else None
    # initial best
    if parent is not None:
       best_h = np.asarray(parent, dtype=float)
       best_n = best_h.size
       # ensure if best_n odd? continuous okay; if want binary even maybe not. exact.
       best_c = exact_c5(best_h,best_n)
       if best_n % 2 != 0:
          # for binary later, but continuous evaluate okay.
          pass
    else:
       best_h = random_soft_start(250,rng); best_n=250; best_c=exact
    # If parent n odd or not even, for continuous use n0 maybe 250? Keep.
    # Phase continuous
    cont_end = min(dl, t0 + 0.22*usable)
    # n choices
    cont_250_end = min(cont_end, t0 + 0.10*usable)
    if parent is not None:
       h0_250 = interpolate_to_n(parent, 250)
    else: h0_250=best_h
    h1_250, c1 = optimize_continuous(h0_250, 250, cont_250_end, seed)
    update best if c1<best_c
    # Also from binary threshold of parent 250
    if time.time() < cont_end - 20:
       hb = make_binary(h0_250,250,rng).astype(float)
       h2,c2 = optimize_continuous(hb,250,cont_250_end,seed+1)
       update
    # n=500 continuous if time
    if time.time() < cont_end - 10:
       h0_500 = interpolate_to_n(best continuous/parent,500)
       h5,c5 = optimize_continuous(h0_500,500,cont_end,seed+2)
       update

Implement:
```

def binary_phase(n, start_list, t_end, base_seed):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(base_seed)
    best_h = None; best_M = None
    # allocate initial chunk: if len starts>0, first_start_end = min(t_end-2, now + max(2, (t_end-now)//max(len(start_list),1)))
    for idx,hstart in enumerate(start_list):
         if time.time() > t_end - 2: break
         # chunk at least 5, but ensure finish by t_end if remaining starts
         remaining_starts = max(len(start_list)-idx, 1)
         if time.time() + 5 < t_end:
            chunk = min(30.0, (t_end - time.time())/(remaining_starts*1.2))
         else: chunk = t_end - time.time() - 1
         if chunk < 0.5: chunk = max(0.2, t_end-time.time()-1)
         sub_end = min(t_end - 1, time.time()+chunk)
         h2,M2= binary_descend(hstart,n,sub_end, base_seed+100*idx, full_allowed=(n<=500), sa_budget=3.0)
         if best_M is None or M2<best_M: best_H=B...
    # restart perturb loop
    attempt=0
    while time.time() < t_end - 5:
       if best_H is None: break
       attempt +=1
       k = min(5 + 2*attempt, n//4)
       hstart = perturb_binary(best_H, k, rng)
       # chunk: smaller for many attempts
       if n <=300: chunk=4.0
       elif n <=500: chunk=15.0
       elif n <=800: chunk=10.0
       else: chunk=8.0
       sub_end = min(t_end - 2, time.time()+chunk)
       h2,M2=binary_descend(hstart,n,sub_end, base_seed+1000+10*attempt, full_allowed=(n<=500), sa_budget=2.0)
       if M2<best_M: update; attempt=0? maybe keep
    if best_h is not None: return best_h, float(best_M)*(2.0/n)
    return None,None
```

def finalize_h(h,n):
   h = project_feasible(h,n)
   target=0.5*n
   diff=target-h.sum()
   if abs(diff) > 0:
       # adjust one interior
       for i in range(n):
           if 1e-12 < h[i] < 1.0-1e-12:
               h[i] += diff
               break
       else:
           h = project_feasible(h,n)
   h = np.clip(h,0,1)
   # if sum still off, redistribute tiny
   diff=target-h.sum()
   if abs(diff) > 1e-14:
       h = project_feasible(h,n)
   return h
```

```
def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0=time.time()
    work=max(60.0,float(budget_s)-5.0)
    dl=t0+float(budget_s)-3.0
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    parent=get_parent_array()
    if parent is not None and parent.size > 0:
        best_h=parent
        best_n=parent.size
        best_c=exact_c5(np.asarray(best_h,float),best_n)
    else:
        best_h=random_soft_start(250,rng); best_n=250; best_c=exact_c5(best_h,best_n)
    # initial sweep
    sweep_end=min(t0+15.0, dl)
    for nh in [250,500,750,1000,1500,2000]:
       if time.time()>sweep_end: break
       if nh==best_n: continue
       hh=interpolate_to_n(best_h,best_n,nh)
       c=exact_c5(hh,nh)
       if c<best_c: best_h=hh; best_c=c; best_n=nh
    # continuous
    cont_end=min(dl, t0 + 0.20*work)
    # 250 continuous
    if parent is not None: src=parent
    else: src=best_h
    if time.time() < cont_end:
       h0=interpolate_to_n(src,250)
       hc,cc=optimize_continuous(h0,250,cont_end,seed)
       if cc<best_c: update
       # binary threshold start continuous
       if time.time() < cont_end - 20:
          hb0=make_binary(hc if hc.size==250 else interpolate_to_n(hc,hc.size,250),250,rng).astype(float)
          hc2,cc2=optimize_continuous(hb0,250,cont_end,seed+1)
          update
    # 500 continuous
    if time.time() < cont_end - 10:
       h0=interpolate_to_n(best_h,best_n,500)
       hc,cc=optimize_continuous(h0,500,cont_end,seed+2)
       update
    # second sweep
    sweep2_end=min(dl, cont_end + 25.0)
    for nh in ...:
       if best_n==nh: continue
       hh=interpolate_to_n(best_h,best_n,nh); c=...
       update
    # binary 250
    n250_end=min(dl, t0 + 0.45*work)
    if time.time() < n250_end:
       starts=[]
       starts.append(make_binary(interpolate_to_n(best_h,best_n,250),250,rng))
       if parent is not None: starts.append(make_binary(interpolate_to_n(parent,parent.size,250),250,rng))
       starts.append(make_binary(random_soft_start(250,rng),250,rng))
       hb,cb=binary_phase(250,starts,n250_end,seed+100)
       if cb is not None and cb<best_c: update; best_binary_250=hb
    # binary 500
    n500_end=min(dl, t0 + 0.90*work)
    if time.time() < n500_end:
       starts=[]
       starts.append(make_binary(interpolate_to_n(best_h,best_n,500),500,rng))
       if parent is not None: starts.append(make_binary(interpolate_to_n(parent,parent.size,500),500,rng))
       if best_binary_250 is not None: starts.append(make_binary(interpolate_to_n(best_binary_250,250,500),500,rng))
       starts.append(make_binary(random_soft_start(500,rng),500,rng))
       hb,cb=binary_phase(500,starts,n500_end,seed+200)
       update; best_binary_500=hb
    # n750 if time
    n750_end=min(dl, time.time() + min(60.0, max(0, dl-time.time())))
    if dl - time.time() > 30:
       starts=[] ... from best_h, best_binary_500 etc
       hb,cb=binary_phase(750,starts,dl,seed+300)
       update
    # n1000 if time
    if dl - time.time() > 40:
       starts ... binary_phase(1000, ...)
    # final maybe evaluation of all candidate? no.
    best_h_out=finalize_h(best_h,best_n)
    best_c_out=exact_c5(best_h_out,best_n)
    return best_h_out, best_c_out, best_n
```

Need `finalize_h` function.
```
def finalize_h(h,n):
    h = project_feasible(np.asarray(h, dtype=float), n)
    # one more tiny correction
    target = 0.5*n
    diff = target - float(h.sum())
    if abs(diff) > 1e-15:
        for i in range(n):
            if 1e-10 < h[i] < 1.0 - 1e-10:
                h[i] += diff
                break
        h = project_feasible(h,n)
    return np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
```
If binary h exact, no diff. If continuous diff small, adjust. If adding diff makes h slightly out of bounds due floating, clip. clip may change sum by tiny; project again maybe. Use `project_feasible` after clip.

def optimize_continuous(h0, n, t_end, seed):
    global G_ALPHA, G_N, G_DX, G_TARGET, G_DEADLINE, G_LAM

def binary_descend(h_init, n, t_end, seed, full_allowed, sa_budget):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    h = np.asarray(h_init, dtype=np.int8).copy()
    c,U,zeros,ones,posz,poso,M = recompute_state(h)
    best_h = h.copy(); best_M = M
    swaps = 0
    while time.time() < t_end:
        use_full = full_allowed and n <= 500
        if use_full:
            za = np.asarray(zeros, dtype=np.intp)
            oa = np.asarray(ones, dtype=np.intp)
            a,b,nm = find_best_swap_full(c,U,za,oa,M)
        else:
            samples = 5000 if n <= 900 else 2500
            a,b,nm = find_best_swap_sampled_lists(c,U,zeros,ones,M,rng,samples)
        if a >= 0:
            M = apply_swap(c,U,h,a,b)
            zeros,ones,posz,poso = remove_insert_lists(...)
            swaps += 1
            if M < best_M:
                best_M = M; best_h = h.copy()
            if swaps % 500 == 0:
                c,U,zeros,ones,posz,poso,M = recompute_state(h)
                if M < best_M: best_M=M; best_h=h.copy()
        else:
            if time.time() < t_end - 1 and sa_budget > 0:
                M_before = M
                t_sa = min(t_end - 0.5, time.time() + sa_budget)
                h,c,U,zeros,ones,posz,poso,M = binary_sa(...)
                if M < best_M:
                    best_M=M; best_h=h.copy()
                if M <= M_before:
                    break
            else:
                break
    final_M = binary_max_count(best_h)
    if final_M < best_M: best_M=final_M
    return best_h, best_M
```

def make_bounds(n):
    b = []
    for i in range(n):

`random_soft_start`:
```
def random_soft_start(n, rng):
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n, endpoint=False)
    f1 = float(rng.randint(2, 8))
    f2 = float(rng.randint(4, 14))
    p1 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 6.283185307179586))
    p2 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 6.283185307179586))
    h = 0.5 + 0.35*np.sin(2.0*np.pi*f1*x + p1) + 0.25*np.sin(2.0*np.pi*f2*x + p2)
    h += 0.08*rng.normal(size=n)
    return project_feasible(h, n)
```
`rng.randint(2,8)` if rng is RandomState returns int. Good.

        return None

`interpolate_to_n`: If `src` is list, `src.size`? Use `m = np.asarray(src).size`. But if src is list, `len` works. Do:
```
def interpolate_to_n(src,n):
    arr=np.asarray(src, dtype=float)
    m=arr.size
    if m==n:
       return project_feasible(arr,n)
    if m==1:
       out=np.full(n,float(arr[0]))
    else:
       xo=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,m,endpoint=False)
       xn=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n,endpoint=False)
       out=np.interp(xn,xo,arr)
    return project_feasible(out,n)
```
Good.

```
def binary_phase(n, start_list, t_end, base_seed):
    if len(start_list)==0: return None,None
    rng = np.random.RandomState(base_seed)
    best_h=None; best_M=None
    # initial starts
    for idx in range(len(start_list)):
       if time.time() > t_end - 1.0: break
       if n <= 300: chunk=6.0
       elif n <= 500: chunk=20.0
       elif n <= 800: chunk=10.0
       else: chunk=8.0
       sub_end = min(t_end - 1.0, time.time() + chunk)
       if sub_end <= time.time(): break
       h2, M2 = binary_descend(start_list[idx], n, sub_end, base_seed+100*idx, n <= 500, 3.0)
       if h2 is not None:
          M2 = binary_max_count(h2)
          if best_M is None or M2 < best_M:
             best_M=M2; best_h=h2
    attempt=0
    while time.time() < t_end - 3.0:
       if best_h is None: break
       attempt += 1
       k = min(5 + 4*attempt, max(1, n//3))
       hstart = perturb_binary(best_h, k, rng)
       if n <= 300: chunk=4.0
       elif n <= 500: chunk=15.0
       elif n <= 800: chunk=8.0
       else: chunk=6.0
       sub_end = min(t_end - 2.0, time.time() + chunk)
       if sub_end <= time.time(): break
       h2,M2 = binary_descend(hstart,n,sub_end, base_seed+1000+7*attempt, n<=500, 2.0)
       if h2 is not None:
          M2=binary_max_count(h2)
          if M2 < best_M:
              best_M=M2; best_h=h2; attempt=0
    if best_h is None:
       # fallback: first start? If no time maybe binary_descend not called? But start_list nonempty and t_end maybe close.
       h2 = start_list[0]
       M2 = binary_max_count(h2)
       best_h=h2; best_M=M2
    c = float(best_M) * (2.0/float(n))
    return best_h, c
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math
from scipy.optimize import minimize

G_ALPHA = 1.0
G_N = 0
G_DX = 1.0
G_TARGET = 0.0
G_LAM = 1.0
G_DEADLINE = 0.0
G_ARANGE = None
G_ARANGE_N = -1


def get_arange(n):
    global G_ARANGE, G_ARANGE_N
    if G_ARANGE_N != n:
        G_ARANGE = np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)
        G_ARANGE_N = n
    return G_ARANGE


def project_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    target = 0.5 * float(n)
    for _ in range(10000):
        s = float(h.sum())
        diff = target - s
        if abs(diff) < 1e-14:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            ss = float(room.sum())
            if ss < 1e-15:
                break
            h = h + (diff / ss) * room
        else:
            ss = s
            if ss < 1e-15:
                break
            h = h + (diff / ss) * h
    return np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)


def interpolate_to_n(src, n):
    arr = np.asarray(src, dtype=float)
    m = int(arr.size)
    n = int(n)
    if m == n:
        return project_feasible(arr, n)
    if m == 1:
        out = np.full(n, float(arr[0]), dtype=float)
        return project_feasible(out, n)
    xo = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, m, endpoint=False)
    xn = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n, endpoint=False)
    out = np.interp(xn, xo, arr)
    return project_feasible(out, n)


def exact_c5(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return float("inf")
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    return float(np.max(c) * dx)


def finalize_h(h, n):
    h = project_feasible(np.asarray(h, dtype=float), n)
    target = 0.5 * float(n)
    diff = target - float(h.sum())
    if abs(diff) > 1e-15:
        for i in range(n):
            if 1e-10 < h[i] < 1.0 - 1e-10:
                h[i] = float(h[i] + diff)
                break
        h = project_feasible(h, n)
    return np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)


def timeout_check():
    if time.time() > G_DEADLINE:
        raise RuntimeError("time budget exceeded")


def sum_constraint(x):
    return float(x.sum() - G_TARGET)


def soft_grad_from_p(x, p):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    n = int(x.size)
    v = 1.0 - x
    h = x
    t1 = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    t2 = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    center = n - 1
    L = int(p.size)
    for idx in range(L):
        w = float(p[idx])
        if w <= 1e-16:
            continue
        d = idx - center
        if d >= 0:
            if d < n:
                t1[:n - d] += w * v[d:]
                t2[d:] += w * h[:n - d]
        else:
            e = -d
            if e < n:
                t1[e:] += w * v[:n - e]
                t2[:n - e] += w * h[e:]
    return G_DX * (t1 - t2)


def soft_objective(x):
    timeout_check()
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    f = np.correlate(x, 1.0 - x, mode="full") * G_DX
    m = float(f.max())
    z = G_ALPHA * (f - m)
    zm = float(z.max())
    ez = np.exp(z - zm)
    s = float(ez.sum())
    return float(m + (zm + math.log(s)) / G_ALPHA)


def soft_gradient(x):
    timeout_check()
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    f = np.correlate(x, 1.0 - x, mode="full") * G_DX
    m = float(f.max())
    z = G_ALPHA * (f - m)
    zm = float(z.max())
    ez = np.exp(z - zm)
    s = float(ez.sum())
    p = ez / s
    return soft_grad_from_p(x, p)


def penalty_objective(x):
    timeout_check()
    val = soft_objective(x)
    diff = float(x.sum() - G_TARGET)
    return val + 0.5 * G_LAM * diff * diff


def penalty_gradient(x):
    timeout_check()
    g = soft_gradient(x)
    diff = float(x.sum() - G_TARGET)
    g += G_LAM * diff
    return g


def make_bounds(n):
    b = []
    for _ in range(n):
        b.append((0.0, 1.0))
    return b


def optimize_continuous(h0, n, t_end, seed):
    global G_ALPHA, G_N, G_DX, G_TARGET, G_DEADLINE, G_LAM

    n = int(n)
    if n < 50 or time.time() > t_end - 5.0:
        h = project_feasible(np.asarray(h0, dtype=float), n)
        return h, exact_c5(h, n)

    h = project_feasible(np.asarray(h0, dtype=float), n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = exact_c5(h, n)

    G_N = n
    G_DX = 2.0 / float(n)
    G_TARGET = 0.5 * float(n)
    G_DEADLINE = max(time.time(), t_end - 2.0)

    bounds = make_bounds(n)
    cons = [{"type": "eq", "fun": sum_constraint}]

    alphas = [50.0, 200.0, 1000.0, 5000.0, 20000.0, 100000.0]
    if n <= 300 and time.time() < t_end - 25.0:
        alphas.append(200000.0)

    if n <= 300:
        maxiter = 700
    elif n <= 600:
        maxiter = 300
    else:
        maxiter = 150

    for alpha in alphas:
        if time.time() > t_end - 8.0:
            break
        G_ALPHA = alpha

        if n <= 350:
            try:
                res = minimize(
                    soft_objective,
                    h,
                    jac=soft_gradient,
                    method="SLSQP",
                    bounds=bounds,
                    constraints=cons,
                    options={"maxiter": maxiter, "ftol": 1e-12, "disp": False},
                )
                if res.x is not None:
                    xnew = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float)
                    if xnew.size == n and np.all(np.isfinite(xnew)):
                        h = project_feasible(xnew, n)
            except Exception:
                pass
        else:
            G_LAM = 1.0e5
            try:
                res = minimize(
                    penalty_objective,
                    h,
                    jac=penalty_gradient,
                    method="L-BFGS-B",
                    bounds=bounds,
                    options={"maxiter": maxiter, "ftol": 1e-12, "disp": False},
                )
                if res.x is not None:
                    xnew = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float)
                    if xnew.size == n and np.all(np.isfinite(xnew)):
                        h = project_feasible(xnew, n)
            except Exception:
                pass

        cnow = exact_c5(h, n)
        if cnow < best_c:
            best_c = cnow
            best_h = h.copy()

    if n <= 350 and time.time() < t_end - 6.0:
        G_LAM = 1.0e5
        try:
            res = minimize(
                penalty_objective,
                h,
                jac=penalty_gradient,
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                bounds=bounds,
                options={"maxiter": max(100, maxiter // 2), "ftol": 1e-12, "disp": False},
            )
            if res.x is not None:
                xnew = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float)
                if xnew.size == n and np.all(np.isfinite(xnew)):
                    h = project_feasible(xnew, n)
        except Exception:
            pass

    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    cnow = exact_c5(h, n)
    if cnow < best_c:
        best_c = cnow
        best_h = h.copy()

    return best_h, best_c


def random_soft_start(n, rng):
    n = int(n)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n, endpoint=False)
    f1 = float(rng.randint(2, 8))
    f2 = float(rng.randint(4, 14))
    p1 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 6.283185307179586))
    p2 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 6.283185307179586))
    h = (
        0.5
        + 0.35 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * f1 * x + p1)
        + 0.25 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * f2 * x + p2)
    )
    h = h + 0.08 * rng.normal(size=n)
    return project_feasible(h, n)


def get_parent_array():
    try:
        return initial_h_values
    except Exception:
        return None


def make_binary(h_float, n, rng):
    h = np.asarray(h_float, dtype=float)
    n = int(n)
    if h.size != n:
        h = interpolate_to_n(h, n)
    target = n // 2
    h = h + 1e-7 * rng.normal(size=n)
    if target <= 0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.int8)
    idx = np.argpartition(h, n - target)
    top = idx[n - target:]
    out = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.int8)
    out[top] = 1
    if int(out.sum()) != target:
        idx = np.argsort(h)[::-1]
        out = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.int8)
        out[idx[:target]] = 1
    return out


def binary_initial_c(h):
    hi = h.astype(np.int64)
    c = np.correlate(hi, 1 - hi, mode="full")
    return c.astype(np.int32, copy=False)


def binary_max_count(h):
    hi = h.astype(np.int64)
    c = np.correlate(hi, 1 - hi, mode="full")
    if c.size == 0:
        return 0
    return int(c.max())


def compute_U(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.int32)
    n = int(h.size)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    v = (1 - h).astype(np.int32)
    U = np.zeros((n, L), dtype=np.int32)
    center = n - 1
    for d in range(-(n - 1), n):
        k = center + d
        if d >= 0:
            U[: n - d, k] += v[d:]
            U[d:, k] -= h[: n - d]
        else:
            e = -d
            U[e:, k] += v[: n - e]
            U[: n - e, k] -= h[e:]
    return U


def init_index_lists(h):
    zeros = []
    ones = []
    posz = {}
    poso = {}
    for i in range(int(h.size)):
        if h[i] == 0:
            posz[i] = len(zeros)
            zeros.append(i)
        else:
            poso[i] = len(ones)
            ones.append(i)
    return zeros, ones, posz, poso


def remove_insert_lists(zeros, ones, posz, poso, a, b):
    ia = posz[a]
    lastz = zeros[-1]
    zeros[ia] = lastz
    posz[lastz] = ia
    zeros.pop()
    del posz[a]

    ib = poso[b]
    lasto = ones[-1]
    ones[ib] = lasto
    poso[lasto] = ib
    ones.pop()
    del poso[b]

    posz[b] = len(zeros)
    zeros.append(b)
    poso[a] = len(ones)
    ones.append(a)
    return zeros, ones, posz, poso


def update_U(U, a, b):
    n = int(U.shape[0])
    xs = get_arange(n)
    center = n - 1
    U[:, center + xs - a] -= 1
    U[:, center + a - xs] -= 1
    U[:, center + xs - b] += 1
    U[:, center + b - xs] += 1


def apply_swap(c, U, h, a, b):
    n = int(h.size)
    center = n - 1
    c += U[a]
    c -= U[b]
    c[center] -= 2
    c[center + b - a] += 1
    c[center + a - b] += 1
    h[a] = 1
    h[b] = 0
    update_U(U, a, b)
    return int(c.max())


def find_best_swap_full(c, U, zeros_arr, ones_arr, M):
    if len(zeros_arr) == 0 or len(ones_arr) == 0:
        return -1, -1, M
    center = int(c.size - 1) // 2
    Uo = U[ones_arr]
    best = int(M)
    besta = -1
    bestb = -1

    for a in zeros_arr:
        base = c + U[a]
        base[center] -= 2
        vals = (base[None, :] - Uo).max(axis=1)
        D = ones_arr - a
        idxD = center + D
        idxM = center - D
        cross = np.maximum(base[idxD] - Uo[:, idxD] + 1, base[idxM] - Uo[:, idxM] + 1)
        tot = np.maximum(vals, cross)
        local = int(np.argmin(tot))
        tv = int(tot[local])
        if tv < best:
            best = tv
            besta = int(a)
            bestb = int(ones_arr[local])
    return besta, bestb, best


def find_best_swap_sampled_lists(c, U, zeros, ones, M, rng, samples):
    if len(zeros) == 0 or len(ones) == 0:
        return -1, -1, M
    center = int(c.size - 1) // 2
    best = int(M)
    besta = -1
    bestb = -1
    nz = len(zeros)
    no = len(ones)
    for _ in range(int(samples)):
        a = zeros[rng.randint(nz)]
        b = ones[rng.randint(no)]
        tmp = c + U[a] - U[b]
        tmp[center] -= 2
        tmp[center + b - a] += 1
        tmp[center + a - b] += 1
        nm = int(tmp.max())
        if nm < best:
            best = nm
            besta = a
            bestb = b
    return besta, bestb, best


def recompute_state(h):
    c = binary_initial_c(h)
    U = compute_U(h)
    zeros, ones, posz, poso = init_index_lists(h)
    M = int(c.max()) if c.size else 0
    return c, U, zeros, ones, posz, poso, M


def binary_sa(h, c, U, zeros, ones, posz, poso, M, t_end, seed):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    n = int(h.size)
    center = n - 1
    start_t = time.time()
    duration = max(1e-6, t_end - start_t)
    T0 = 1.0
    Tmin = 0.05
    bestM = int(M)
    best_h = h.copy()

    while time.time() < t_end:
        if len(zeros) == 0 or len(ones) == 0:
            break
        a = zeros[rng.randint(len(zeros))]
        b = ones[rng.randint(len(ones))]
        D = b - a

        c += U[a]
        c -= U[b]
        c[center] -= 2
        c[center + D] += 1
        c[center - D] += 1
        newM = int(c.max())
        dm = newM - M
        accept = False

        if dm < 0:
            accept = True
        elif dm == 0:
            accept = rng.rand() < 0.35
        else:
            elapsed = max(0.0, time.time() - start_t)
            frac = min(1.0, elapsed / duration)
            T = T0 * (Tmin / T0) ** frac
            if dm <= 3:
                prob = math.exp(-float(dm) / max(T, 1e-6))
                accept = rng.rand() < prob

        if accept:
            M = newM
            h[a] = 1
            h[b] = 0
            update_U(U, a, b)
            zeros, ones, posz, poso = remove_insert_lists(zeros, ones, posz, poso, a, b)
            if M < bestM:
                bestM = M
                best_h = h.copy()
        else:
            c -= U[a]
            c += U[b]
            c[center] += 2
            c[center + D] -= 1
            c[center - D] -= 1

    c, U, zeros, ones, posz, poso, M = recompute_state(best_h)
    return best_h, c, U, zeros, ones, posz, poso, M


def binary_descend(h_init, t_end, seed, full_allowed, sa_budget):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    h = np.asarray(h_init, dtype=np.int8).copy()
    n = int(h.size)
    c, U, zeros, ones, posz, poso, M = recompute_state(h)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_M = int(M)
    swaps = 0

    while time.time() < t_end:
        use_full = bool(full_allowed) and n <= 500
        if use_full:
            za = np.asarray(zeros, dtype=np.intp)
            oa = np.asarray(ones, dtype=np.intp)
            a, b, nm = find_best_swap_full(c, U, za, oa, M)
        else:
            samples = 5000 if n <= 900 else 2500
            a, b, nm = find_best_swap_sampled_lists(c, U, zeros, ones, M, rng, samples)

        if a >= 0:
            M = apply_swap(c, U, h, a, b)
            zeros, ones, posz, poso = remove_insert_lists(zeros, ones, posz, poso, a, b)
            swaps += 1
            if M < best_M:
                best_M = M
                best_h = h.copy()
            if swaps % 200 == 0:
                c, U, zeros, ones, posz, poso, M = recompute_state(h)
                if M < best_M:
                    best_M = M
                    best_h = h.copy()
        else:
            if time.time() < t_end - 1.0 and sa_budget > 0.0:
                M_before = M
                t_sa = min(t_end - 0.5, time.time() + sa_budget)
                h, c, U, zeros, ones, posz, poso, M = binary_sa(
                    h, c, U, zeros, ones, posz, poso, M, t_sa, seed + swaps
                )
                if M < best_M:
                    best_M = M
                    best_h = h.copy()
                if M <= M_before:
                    break
            else:
                break

    finalM = binary_max_count(best_h)
    if finalM < best_M:
        best_M = finalM
    return best_h, best_M


def perturb_binary(h, k, rng):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.int8).copy()
    zeros, ones, posz, poso = init_index_lists(h)
    k = int(k)
    for _ in range(k):
        if len(zeros) == 0 or len(ones) == 0:
            break
        a = zeros[rng.randint(len(zeros))]
        b = ones[rng.randint(len(ones))]
        h[a] = 1
        h[b] = 0
        zeros, ones, posz, poso = remove_insert_lists(zeros, ones, posz, poso, a, b)
    return h


def binary_phase(n, start_list, t_end, base_seed):
    n = int(n)
    if len(start_list) == 0:
        return None, None

    rng = np.random.RandomState(base_seed)
    best_h = None
    best_M = None

    for idx in range(len(start_list)):
        if time.time() > t_end - 1.0:
            break
        if n <= 300:
            chunk = 6.0
        elif n <= 500:
            chunk = 20.0
        elif n <= 800:
            chunk = 10.0
        else:
            chunk = 8.0
        sub_end = min(t_end - 1.0, time.time() + chunk)
        if sub_end <= time.time():
            break
        h2, M2 = binary_descend(start_list[idx], sub_end, base_seed + 100 * idx, n <= 500, 3.0)
        if h2 is not None:
            M2 = binary_max_count(h2)
            if best_M is None or M2 < best_M:
                best_M = M2
                best_h = h2

    attempt = 0
    while time.time() < t_end - 3.0:
        if best_h is None:
            break
        attempt += 1
        k = min(5 + 4 * attempt, max(1, n // 3))
        hstart = perturb_binary(best_h, k, rng)

        if n <= 300:
            chunk = 4.0
        elif n <= 500:
            chunk = 15.0
        elif n <= 800:
            chunk = 8.0
        else:
            chunk = 6.0
        sub_end = min(t_end - 2.0, time.time() + chunk)
        if sub_end <= time.time():
            break

        h2, M2 = binary_descend(hstart, sub_end, base_seed + 1000 + 7 * attempt, n <= 500, 2.0)
        if h2 is not None:
            M2 = binary_max_count(h2)
            if M2 < best_M:
                best_M = M2
                best_h = h2
                attempt = 0

    if best_h is None:
        best_h = start_list[0]
        best_M = binary_max_count(best_h)

    best_c = float(best_M) * (2.0 / float(n))
    return best_h, best_c


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    budget_s = float(budget_s)
    work = max(60.0, budget_s - 5.0)
    deadline = t0 + budget_s - 3.0
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    parent = get_parent_array()
    if parent is not None and len(parent) > 0:
        best_h = parent
        best_n = int(len(parent))
        best_c = exact_c5(best_h, best_n)
    else:
        best_h = random_soft_start(250, rng)
        best_n = 250
        best_c = exact_c5(best_h, best_n)

    # Initial resolution sweep from the supplied construction.
    sweep_end = min(deadline, t0 + 15.0)
    for nh in [250, 500, 750, 1000, 1500, 2000]:
        if time.time() > sweep_end:
            break
        if nh == best_n:
            continue
        hh = interpolate_to_n(best_h, best_n, nh)
        c = exact_c5(hh, nh)
        if c < best_c:
            best_h = hh
            best_n = nh
            best_c = c

    # Continuous polishing.  Analytic gradients for a smoothed max overlap.
    cont_end = min(deadline, t0 + 0.20 * work)
    cont250_end = min(cont_end, t0 + 0.09 * work)

    source250 = parent if parent is not None else best_h
    if time.time() < cont_end - 5.0:
        h0 = interpolate_to_n(source250, 250)
        hc, cc = optimize_continuous(h0, 250, cont250_end, int(seed))
        if cc < best_c:
            best_h = hc
            best_n = 250
            best_c = cc

        if time.time() < cont250_end - 15.0:
            hb0 = make_binary(interpolate_to_n(hc, hc.size, 250), 250, rng).astype(float)
            hc2, cc2 = optimize_continuous(hb0, 250, cont250_end, int(seed) + 1)
            if cc2 < best_c:
                best_h = hc2
                best_n = 250
                best_c = cc2

    if time.time() < cont_end - 10.0:
        h0500 = interpolate_to_n(best_h, best_n, 500)
        hc500, cc500 = optimize_continuous(h0500, 500, cont_end, int(seed) + 2)
        if cc500 < best_c:
            best_h = hc500
            best_n = 500
            best_c = cc500

    # Second resolution sweep after continuous optimization.
    sweep2_end = min(deadline, cont_end + 25.0)
    for nh in [250, 500, 750, 1000, 1500, 2000]:
        if time.time() > sweep2_end:
            break
        if nh == best_n:
            continue
        hh = interpolate_to_n(best_h, best_n, nh)
        c = exact_c5(hh, nh)
        if c < best_c:
            best_h = hh
            best_n = nh
            best_c = c

    # Binary local search at 250 points: cheap exhaustive 2-swap best-improvement.
    n250_end = min(deadline, t0 + 0.45 * work)
    best_binary_250 = None
    if time.time() < n250_end:
        starts250 = []
        starts250.append(make_binary(interpolate_to_n(best_h, best_n, 250), 250, rng))
        if parent is not None:
            starts250.append(make_binary(interpolate_to_n(parent, best_n if best_n > 0 else 250, 250), 250, rng))
        starts250.append(make_binary(random_soft_start(250, rng), 250, rng))

        hb250, cb250 = binary_phase(250, starts250, n250_end, int(seed) + 100)
        if cb250 is not None and cb250 < best_c:
            best_h = hb250
            best_n = 250
            best_c = cb250
        if hb250 is not None:
            best_binary_250 = hb250

    # Binary local search at 500 points: integer max-count 95 gives C5 = 0.380.
    n500_end = min(deadline, t0 + 0.90 * work)
    best_binary_500 = None
    if time.time() < n500_end:
        starts500 = []
        starts500.append(make_binary(interpolate_to_n(best_h, best_n, 500), 500, rng))
        if parent is not None:
            starts500.append(make_binary(interpolate_to_n(parent, best_n if best_n > 0 else 250, 500), 500, rng))
        if best_binary_250 is not None:
            starts500.append(make_binary(interpolate_to_n(best_binary_250, 250, 500), 500, rng))
        starts500.append(make_binary(random_soft_start(500, rng), 500, rng))

        hb500, cb500 = binary_phase(500, starts500, n500_end, int(seed) + 200)
        if cb500 is not None and cb500 < best_c:
            best_h = hb500
            best_n = 500
            best_c = cb500
        if hb500 is not None:
            best_binary_500 = hb500

    # Optional 750-point sampled search if time remains.
    if deadline - time.time() > 30.0:
        starts750 = []
        starts750.append(make_binary(interpolate_to_n(best_h, best_n, 750), 750, rng))
        if best_binary_500 is not None:
            starts750.append(make_binary(interpolate_to_n(best_binary_500, 500, 750), 750, rng))
        starts750.append(make_binary(random_soft_start(750, rng), 750, rng))
        hb750, cb750 = binary_phase(750, starts750, deadline, int(seed) + 300)
        if cb750 is not None and cb750 < best_c:
            best_h = hb750
            best_n = 750
            best_c = cb750

    # Optional 1000-point sampled search if time remains.
    if deadline - time.time() > 35.0:
        starts1000 = []
        starts1000.append(make_binary(interpolate_to_n(best_h, best_n, 1000), 1000, rng))
        if best_binary_500 is not None:
            starts1000.append(make_binary(interpolate_to_n(best_binary_500, 500, 1000), 1000, rng))
        hb1000, cb1000 = binary_phase(1000, starts1000, deadline, int(seed) + 400)
        if cb1000 is not None and cb1000 < best_c:
            best_h = hb1000
            best_n = 1000
            best_c = cb1000

    best_out = finalize_h(best_h, best_n)
    best_c_out = exact_c5(best_out, best_n)
    return best_out, best_c_out, best_n
```
